- **ask-committee**: Put variants, a design, a plan or a diff before a committee of reviewer agents in a design and an engineering chapter. Members vote alone, leads merge each chapter's votes, and the chair writes one verdict with every vote attached. Use when the user asks the committee or wants a committee verdict. - **ask-company**: Ask the whole company a question through Employee001 and get one answer, citing which colleague or document each fact came from.
